ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u332	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Polish Free and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the parliamentary draft resolution on commemorating the 160th anniversary of the birth of Teodor Axentowicz. Theodor Axentowicz is one of the most outstanding Polish artists of the turn of the 19th and 20th centuries. He was born on 13 May 1859 in Brasov in an Armenian family. He was a painter, a sketch artist and a graphic designer. He was educated at the Academy of Fine Arts in Munich under Gabriel Hackl, Alexander Wagner, Gyuli Benczura. Axentowicz became famous primarily as the author of idealized female images perfectly performed in pastel techniques and children's portraits. The subjects that appeared in his paintings were folk rituals of people living in the Hucul region - funerals, religious processes, and rural fun and dances. The world-famous painter has left behind an extremely rich creative work that is appreciated by both visitors to museums and private collectors. As was underlined at the meeting of the Committee on Culture and Media, the draft resolution was the result of the Armenian Association's initiative. Archbishop. Józef Theofil Theodorowicz, as the representative of the applicants rightly noted during the meeting, is valuable because he demonstrates the active involvement of Polish Armenians in the life of Poland. The Free and Solidarni Circle supports the draft resolution presented.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u333	We will proceed to the vote on this motion for a resolution in the voting block. We proceed with the examination of item 37. of the agenda: First reading of the government draft law amending the Act on VAT on goods and services and some other laws (print No 3499). Undersecretary of State in the Ministry of Finance Mr Filip Świtała will present the reasons for the bill. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u334	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Sealing the tax system and ensuring greater stability of tax revenue, including mainly VAT and excise duty, has been and remains in recent years one of the important actions taken by the government. The aim of the proposed law is to introduce additional solutions to seal the VAT and excise systems and to clarify the measures already taken in this area. In addition, the proposal proposes simplifying the law and removing some unnecessary and burdensome obligations for taxpayers. Changes are also foreseen in the Energy Law, which are correlated with the changes in VAT and excise duties, in order to improve efficiency of so-called. the fuel package. The project also implements one of the judgments of the Court of Justice of the European Union. The regulations introduced will also strengthen fair competition among entrepreneurs and reduce the grey economy, which will positively affect business conditions. The first major change concerns the fuel package. The fuel package entered into force on 1 August 2016. Its main objective was to introduce solutions to prevent or restrict fraud in the circulation of liquid fuels. Changes in the so-called the fuel package provided for in this project consists essentially of the introduction into the VAT Act of a catalogue of goods for which the taxable person is obliged without calling on the head of the tax office to calculate and pay, within an accelerated period, the amounts of the tax on goods and services to the tax office. The definition of this catalogue of goods will prevent doubts as to the scope of the VAT Act and the Excise Tax Act. The changes are also due to the need to correlate the scope of the so-called application. accelerated VAT refund with the concession scope, as well as additional addition to the catalogue for reference to three additional categories, i.e. for liquid fuels within the meaning of the Law on the monitoring and control of the quality of fuels, for liquid biofuels within the meaning of the Law on liquid biocomponents and biofuels and for the category of motor fuels under the Law on excise duty. This is intended to include the so-called mechanism. an accelerated VAT refund for all energy products which would be used for internal combustion engines, including in a way that does not comply with the rules. The proposed amendment will generally not increase the obligations on taxpayers, those who purchase goods not currently covered by the definition of motor fuels, and those related to the amount of the tax on goods and services to be paid, but will only shift the obligation to pay over time. An important element regulated by this project is the modification of the principle of issuing invoices on the basis of so-called. a fiscal receipt. Invoices are currently being issued on the basis of collected receipts, i.e. collect receipts left by other customers and issue invoices which are later used as proof of deduction of VAT and increase the cost of obtaining revenue. The amendment introduces the principle that in the case of sales registered using a register, i.e. when a receipt is issued, the tax identification number of the taxable person making the purchase should be entered in this receipt. For an exhibitor who will not comply with this obligation, and for a buyer who will benefit from such an invoice and who has not previously purchased the goods for a receipt with his NIP number, there will be a penalty of 100% in both cases. VAT. This project extends the catalogue of taxable persons for whom no VAT exemption applies. Subjective exemption, which currently amounts to 40 000. The euro, is abused in some so-called. sensitive industries. The changes will apply to taxable persons supplying parts of motor vehicles and motorcycles, regardless of how the delivery is carried out, but in particular we are talking about trade in car parts on the Internet, as well as taxable persons supplying cosmetic and toilet preparations, computers, electronic equipment, RTV and household appliances and other such goods in connection with the conclusion of a contract under an organised system of distance contracts, namely again on the Internet, and taxpayers providing debt collection services, including factoring. This limitation is linked to our analysis, which shows that there is abuse in these industries in the form of the use of this individual exemption. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The regulations for flat-rate farmers are also to be revised. It is envisaged to amend the data on documents issued for the purchase of agricultural products and agricultural services from flat-rate farmers. This amendment concerns the elimination of the obligation to include on VAT invoices certain data on documents identifying suppliers of agricultural products, as well as the possibility of issuing and sending these documents electronically. In addition, it is intended to modify the conditions for the deduction of the flat-rate tax by purchasers of agricultural products and agricultural services from flat-rate farmers by eliminating the condition in the form of payment for purchased goods or services within 14 days and introducing the possibility of making such payment also on the account of the cooperative savings and credit bank of which the farmer is a member. To date, this obligation to pay was only applicable to the bank accounts of the farmer. This change is due to the well-established case law of the administrative courts, indicating that the provisions are not compatible with the EU VAT Directive. It should be pointed out that the amendments to the rules on registration of taxable persons are partly intended to clarify the existing rules, but are also intended to improve the updating of the VAT register, which is such an important tool for taxable persons who assess the reliability of their counterparties on a daily basis. For example, it is worth pointing out that conditions are added in the form of an obligation to apply for the re-registration of VAT no later than 2 months after the date of deletion and, in the case of non-submission of a declaration despite such an obligation, if, at the latest, the applicant submits all the missing declarations. The most important changes in VAT are the following: the proposal also provides for certain facilitations for bailiffs and enforcement authorities concerning the determination of the debtor's VAT status. The project provides for taxpayers to reduce the amount of the additional VAT obligation to 15% where the taxable person pays, he shall make an adjustment to the tax return after the initiation of the customs-subsistence check. The draft also provides for the implementation of the judgment of the TSUE in Case C308/16, i.e. Kozuba Premium Selection. It consists of modifying the conditions for the application of exemptions for the supply of buildings, buildings and parts thereof. The proposed project also seals the trade in excise goods which are not subject to harmonised excise duties within the European Union, but in Poland are taxed by excise duty and subject to suspension of excise duty. This is to eliminate irregularities occurring in the marketing of these products. The amendment provides for an extension of the conditions for applying the suspension of excise duty when moving these products within the territory of the country in the context of intra-Community acquisitions or intra-Community supplies. Following changes in the acquisition of intra-Community excise goods, such as lubrication oils, an additional condition for them to be covered in the country by the suspension of excise duty shall be that the taxable person makes a notification of the intended acquisition of the intra-Community goods to the competent head of the tax office and lodges an excise security. In the case of intra-Community supply, an additional condition for the movement of the above products under suspension of excise duty shall be the lodging of an excise security. It is also necessary to introduce an effective rate of excise duty on lubricants, synthetic preparations, classified as CN 3403, used so far by entities operating in the so-called. the grey zone in order to avoid the tax obligation for mineral lubricants. It's about equalizing the status of these two types of oils. In addition, it is envisaged to introduce in the Excise Tax Act amendments that clarify and seal the CN classification of energy products, introduced in the Excise Tax Act from 1 January 2019 under the Law of 9 November 2018 amending certain laws with a view to introducing simplifications for traders in tax and economic law. The following project also amends the Energy Law Act. The definition of liquid fuels is to be clarified. This is about comparing these changes with the changes that we provide for in the VAT Act and the excise Act. The proposed regulations will have a positive financial impact on the state budget. In the first year of implementation of the envisaged measures, approximately PLN 51.5 million should be paid to the budget, and in the following years it will be over PLN 600 million per year. The total revenue by 2029 should be about PLN 6.1 billion. As far as expenditure is concerned, we assume that the planned tasks are carried out as a rule within the framework of current financial resources. The exclusion of certain taxable persons in the case of VAT from the personal exemption I mentioned will increase the number of obligations. It's about keeping records, making declarations or issuing invoices. This may entail additional costs. There will be an increase in the administrative burden on companies resulting from the inclusion of lubricants in the suspension of excise duties procedure. We assume that the proposed legislation will enter into force on 1 September 2019. There are also some exceptions - some provisions will enter into force on the day following the date of publication of the bill. These include: certain excise goods and lubricating oils of CN heading 3403. Finally, I would like to emphasise once again that the regulations introduced will not only seal the tax system and, as a result, further reduce the VAT gap, but also improve the operating conditions. In particular, it is about increasing the level of fair competition in economic trade by reducing the unauthorised advantage that tax fraud operators have achieved so far over honest taxpayers. In view of the above, I kindly ask the High House to refer this project to further parliamentary work. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u352	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The draft law amending the Act on VAT on goods and services and certain other laws, print no. 3500, introduces a significant change to make life easier for taxpayers. Namely, we simplify the method of accounting by consolidating the VAT return with the JPK_VAT file. At the moment, the situation is that traders who pay VAT, VAT payers must submit VAT returns and also JPK_VAT, i.e. monthly reporting of VAT sales and purchases in the JPK format. We will consolidate these obligations so that, after the change, taxpayers can send one file consisting of a declaration and containing data from the declaration and VAT records. This will benefit most taxpayers. It will simplify life and, above all, one document will become a more complete document which will remove the burdensome obligations to attach annexes, such as the VAT refund application, the information on the use of the relief for bad debts and the request to speed up the tax refund deadline. The information from these annexes will already be included in the JPK_VAT file and the tax office will take it into account and the taxpayer will not have to attach these documents specifically to his statement. From 1 January 2020, we would like this new obligation to submit a single document to large taxpayers, and then after 6 months, i.e. from 1 July 2020, to be extended to all other taxpayers, so that small, medium-sized and micro-enterprises have much more time to prepare for this new obligation. We assume that from 1 July 2020 it will not be possible to submit a VAT return in any other way than through this single file, through the format of a single control file. The information contained in the VAT return will be transferred to the Poltax Central System, i.e. this new format will make this data into the Poltax Central System and significantly improve the functioning of the system. The data already influences there now, while they will be a little better arranged. The project does not close quarterly accounts, they remain in force, but the quarterly clearing will have to submit VAT records for the first 2 months of each quarter. Unfortunately, it is necessary to maintain the consistency and tightness of the system. We will be able to use data from these new files to a slightly better extent in the Central Register of Taxation Data. We will impose fines for a misdemeanor of not submitting such data by electronic means or not in accordance with the model of the electronic document. We will also impose administrative sanctions for errors in files that prevent their analysis. We allow you to correct these errors: if you make a mistake, you will be notified and after notification you will be able to quickly correct and then everything will be okay, there will be no sanctions. We will reduce the excess in the context of the formalities introduced which are imposed on taxable persons as regards the need to provide customs authorities with documents confirming the payment of import taxes in the tax return. This change aims to eliminate the excessive burden on taxpayers. Because we already introduced it in 2017. The National Tax Administration, thanks to these reforms has become better, is more consolidated, there is a better flow of information. It's just one organism that functions like one organization, one fist that can do anything, has all the information in one place. As a result, the database of tax offices and customs offices has been merged and the information held by the services of the new National Tax Administration is much better. Thus, we can relieve taxpayers from passing on certain data, because we already have it, and we will not ask taxpayers to send these data to us, so we will free them from excessive responsibilities. Another change is to make available to the taxpayer an individual tax account by which payments of personal income tax, corporate tax, goods and services tax and non-tax duties constituting the revenue of the State budget will be made. We have also made some minor changes that are less important, but those that concern the regulation on the payment of tax liabilities whose payment deadline has expired. They shall be counted against the liability at the earliest payment date specified by the taxable person and, in the absence of such indication or absence of arrears in the specified tax, the arrears of the oldest payment date among all the taxpayer's arrears. We also intend to introduce a solution to support banks and cooperative savings and credit banks in the proper performance of their obligations under the Law of 1 March on Anti-Money Laundering and Terrorist Financing. We anticipate that the Act should enter into force on 1 January 2020. This law does not have a significant impact on the functioning of entrepreneurs. On the business side, we will actually make it easier, they will obviously have to adapt their financial and accounting systems to the proposed changes, but the expenditure related to the introduction of this solution, i.e. mainly the cost of adapting IT systems to new solutions, will depend on the size of the entrepreneur, the way in which the books are kept or whether the changes will require only the accounting system or the management system of the whole company. It can also be assumed that they will be 0 PLN for those entrepreneurs who wish to use the free tools prepared by the Ministry of Finance as solutions primarily for micro-entrepreneurs. The scope of the changes will require much less investment than the implementation of the previous JPK scheme, because in a large part they are the same data that are already being submitted at the moment. VAT returns are also made by electronic means at the moment. As regards the state budget, the costs associated with the modification of the current reporting system, VAT-7 and VAT-7K declarations and their submission and analysis in the Central Tax Data Repository will amount to around 12, almost 13 million - 12,822 thousand. zł. The expenditure on the application part of these costs will amount to 2822 000. zł. In addition to this amount, the cost of the infrastructure part, which is estimated at the moment is about PLN 10 million. In the following years 2020,2029, the maintenance of the system is expected to generate costs of around 300 000. PLN per year with a total cost of approx. 3 million PLN by 2029. Therefore, the total amount of expenditure related to the modification and maintenance of the scheme for 20192029 should be 15,822 000. zł. It is a certain cost, but we also know that it is a cost associated with really big simplifications for entrepreneurs. At the moment, it is difficult to determine to what extent the proposed solutions will reduce the fraud associated with the settlement of the tax on goods and services. As I said, in some ways the analysis of the data we receive will improve, but it will continue to be the same data that we already have. They are likely to reduce the scale of the so-called exposure. empty invoices and carousel fraud, unfortunately it is difficult to say to what extent. At the same time, a change in the obligation to make declarations by replacing this procedure with a single obligation to send declarations together with records will mainly reduce the burden on taxpayers and will not affect the revenue of the State budget. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I ask the High House to support this project and to direct it to work in the Committee on Public Finance. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u370	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! I present a draft law amending the Law on Cleanliness and Order in municipalities and some other laws. The aim of this project is above all to reduce the unjustified increase in prices for the collection and management of municipal waste that we have seen in recent years. The Act also aims to provide incentives for residents to collect municipal waste separately and to further reduce irregularities in municipal waste management. I say: further reduction of irregularities, because in waste management laws adopted in July ub. These irregularities were largely excluded. What are our proposals and the most important solutions for amending this law? So, first of all, I think this is the most important change that can affect prices - the abolition of the obligation to transfer waste to installations within the region, i.e. the abolition of regionalisation. This will increase competitiveness and eliminate monopolistic practices, which unfortunately we can now see. Because how else to explain the increase in prices of municipal waste reception in RIPOKs from December 2017 to January 2019 sometimes by more than 100%. Of course, labour costs are rising, perhaps others, but they do not increase by more than 100% by any means. Another solution is to propose a reduction in the fees for waste management from the financial resources from the sale of secondary raw materials that have been selectively collected. Municipalities will be able to fund waste finance, but from revenues from the sale of secondary raw materials, not from their own budget. Although I admit, there were proposals from municipalities to do so directly from the municipality's budget, from their own income. This may be an incentive, on the one hand, for residents to collect separately, on the other hand, also an incentive for municipalities to organise selective collection efficiently, because the funds thus obtained can be used to reduce the charges for residents. Another proposal is the exemption from the part of municipal waste management fees of property owners who use biodegradable waste in home composters. So if someone doesn't give away biodegradable waste, they have their composter, they'll have a chance to lower the rate of the waste charge. We also propose a revision of the rules for setting the rate of charge for municipal waste management from the holiday house, the property occupied for recreational and leisure purposes. There have been conclusions that these charges have been abused and raised very strongly. Hence the proposal to limit them from the top. So no more than 10% for a holiday home. the average monthly income available per person, i.e. at the moment it is approx. 160 PLN. Such a limitation would also be in the case of municipal waste from uninhabited real estate as regards the value of containers. So the limit for a container of 1100 l is not more than 3% per month. monthly disposable income, i.e. approx. 51 zł, and for 120 l bag - per month the maximum rate is 1% , i.e. approx. 16 PLN. We also propose strengthening incentives for selective collection of waste, i.e. increasing the quality of secondary raw materials for recycling through proper selective collection of waste. The segregators will pay less. I mean, you could say that people who don't segregate waste will pay a double fee, a kind of punishment for not collecting the waste selectively. So far it has been any strategy of the municipality. Sometimes these differences were very symbolic. Another thing is to reduce market failures in municipal waste management. So this is about abolishing the possibility of flat-rate settlement of municipalities with entrepreneurs receiving municipal waste from residents and changing to quantitative settlement. This gives municipalities both the opportunity to control the waste stream and also encourages selective collection. The point is that these selectively collected waste should not be included in the mass of the general waste, of the residual waste, of the mixed waste. Another proposal is to change the method for calculating waste recycling levels by municipalities. To date, only fractions have been included in recycling: paper, metal, plastics and glass, while at the moment other fractions from which waste can be used as secondary raw materials will be possible. This new method will allow real calculation of the level of recycling achieved for all waste. This will also encourage you to increase the level of selective collection. If this more general method had already been applied at the moment, taking into account the data for 2017, Poland could boast about 40% of the level of selective collection - the level of preparation for re-use and recycling of municipal waste - in turn using this method, which distinguishes only four fractions, this level in 2017 indicated only 29%. Another proposal is to ban the incineration of mixed municipal waste collected in the municipality, which does not introduce uniform rules for selective collection of waste. We want to avoid such a situation that the municipality, not wanting to introduce a selective collection of waste at all, spends all municipal waste to burn. We recognise, in accordance with the Waste Directive, that incineration is the last resort for waste management. The first is the re-use of secondary raw materials, i.e. recycling. The second - burning, if you can't do anything about the waste. And the final step, the final step, is to dispose of the waste. In accordance with European Union requirements, we are expected to reach 65% in 2035. recycling against other waste, i.e. We're going to reach at least 65%. recycling and not more than 10% We can store the waste. Such solutions, such stimulations are necessary for introduction. The project also introduces some changes in waste management - not only concerning the Cleanliness and Order Act in municipalities - which result from observed phenomena. We want, among other things. improve the possibility for competent environmental authorities to dispose of waste without delay due to a risk to human life or health. Thus, without waiting for the call and the reaction of the owner of the waste, in the case of waste that threatens the health or life of the body, this replacement could be implemented immediately. Of course, the obligation to reimburse the costs of disposal of these waste by their owner, with the introduction of a new possibility to support local authorities from the funds of NFOŚ, is not so much a statutory provision as a technical solution and a technical offer addressed to local authorities to undertake such replacement operations by means of funding from the National Fund for Environmental Protection and Water Management. I also think that it is very important, very important, to extend the responsibility of the hazardous waste manufacturer for the management of the waste until it is recovered or disposed of. It's not like if the manufacturer of hazardous waste hands them over to anyone, his responsibility disappears. Unfortunately, we have observed many times that hazardous waste is passed on to virtually non-existent companies, fictional companies. This waste is subsequently discarded and the producer of the waste is not responsible, but it is difficult to enforce the costs of the disposal of the waste from the company that stored and abandoned the waste. And one more solution, maybe not directly related to the Cleanliness and Order Act, but related to waste management. This is to include a recycling charge for plastic bags with a material thickness of more than 50 micrometers. This recycling charge on plastic bags for purchases was introduced, but for bags from 15-50 micrometers, which showed in practice, taking into account the data for last year, that it was actually a leaky solution due to the fact that the shopping networks, shops have switched to bags thicker than 50 micrometers without any charge. By introducing universal fees, we will avoid circumvention of the law and fleeing payment of the fee. But, of course, the main motive for introducing this fee is to encourage you to give up your bags. We encourage you to give up plastic bags for shopping as far as possible and use re-use bags. We have therefore also found that there is no effective supervision and control regarding these bags. Therefore, we want to introduce monthly toll collection, BDO reporting, so that this commitment, these recycling charges, are not determined only on the basis of a non-verifying declaration introducing foil bags to the market, so that this is strictly controlled. These are the main principles of this law. The bill has been discussed for a very long time. It was based on compromises between entrepreneurs, employers and local authorities responsible mainly for the waste collection system. Therefore, the records evolved in different places depending on the rations of particular stakeholders. I hope that this proposal is a suitable compromise between the expectations of local governments and entrepreneurs, it will lead to a better waste collection system - this is what local authorities are obliged to do - and over time it will also eliminate monopolistic practices and will lead to a real reduction in prices for residents or at least to a halt to their growth. I would like to point out that this really is another step in ordering waste management. Before us is the implementation of a full waste directive, which, including, I hope, adopted today during the vote on waste bases, the law will fully clean up waste management. The Directive, together with the regulations adopted last year, will create a system of comprehensive solutions and put waste management system in order. This relates primarily to environmental benefits, but not only. This will also bring financial benefits to residents, consumers supplying waste. Of course, we require them first of all to be involved in a fair and effective selective collection of these waste. That's the main purpose of this bill. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u371	Thank you, Minister. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. Mr Marek Kwitek, the Law and Justice Club, will be the first to speak.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u372	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I have the honour to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice towards the government draft law amending the Law on Cleanliness and Order in municipalities and some other laws, print No 3495. The amendment introduces a number of changes which were proposed by local governments, residents and municipal entities and entrepreneurs. The aim of the proposed solutions is to seal the existing rules and improve the level of enforcement by authorities, which should have a positive impact on competitiveness and entrepreneurship in the waste management sector. In order to do this, it is necessary to strengthen the control of municipalities over municipal waste management systems and the supervision of waste collection companies. The new solution is that the municipalities will not be able to settle lump sum with garbage collection companies. This made it difficult for local governments to control the municipal waste stream and led to an increase in the costs of operating the system, among others. by overestimating the costs of receiving these waste. The amendment will allow local governments to account for garbage companies, e.g. on the basis of the weight of the waste which has been transferred for processing. Separation of calls for tenders for the collection and procurement of waste will also allow entrepreneurs who do not have a waste management infrastructure to participate in the tenders. This has so far been necessary and has reduced the competitiveness of the operators with the combined invitations to tender. The amendment also encourages selective collection of municipal waste - it introduces a rigid price difference for segregate and non-segregating waste. Those who don't segregate garbage will pay twice as much for them. Increasing the efficiency of selective collection of waste is a prerequisite for improving recycling rates both in terms of the quantity of sorted waste and in terms of the quality of segregation itself. The draft amendment introduces provisions aimed at reducing fees for owners of single-family homes who have composters in which some of the waste arising in the real estate will be processed - biowaste. Decisions on this issue will be taken by the municipalities by a resolution. The amendments also apply to the rules for setting an annual lump sum trash levy on holiday homes and properties used for recreational and leisure purposes. This will contribute to the reduction of the fee rate - it will not be higher than 10% the average monthly income available per person per year. In the case of family gardens, the rubbish levy is to be fixed in the same way as in the case of immovable property in which municipal waste is generated. The new regulations will allow, among others. for the immediate disposal of hazardous waste or human health by the competent environmental authorities at the expense of their owners. A very important change is the abolition of the obligation of regionalisation in terms of development, among others. Biowaste, mixed municipal waste or sorting residues. This will allow the transfer of waste to installations across the country. The co-ownership of waste generators and subsequent owners for the management of waste is also introduced. The amendment of the law tightens the penalties for the management of waste not in accordance with the rules. The project introduced the possibility for the municipality's board to adopt a resolution on own resources subsidies - obtained from the sale by the municipality of secondary raw materials - to the rates of charges for municipal waste management for owners of real estate in the household part, which may prevent increases in rates of charges for residents. The municipality will also be able to lay down additional conditions to facilitate the selective collection of waste by disabled persons, in particular visually impaired persons, which will have a positive impact on the situation of those persons. Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! The proposed amendment aims to simplify the functioning of the waste management system while reducing the burden on residents. It aims to achieve the objectives of EU legislation - to minimise waste generated and preserve as long as possible the value of products, materials and raw materials. The proposed changes will have a positive impact on the environment, health and human life and will contribute to reducing irregularities in waste management. Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! Parliamentary Club Law and Justice is in favour of further proceding this bill. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u400	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! To answer this question, I must say that this is a completely thought-out arrangement. They should be corrected by the same judge who made those mistakes. It is a system known in the west of Europe, it is a system known in Austria, it is a system that defends itself and which works perfectly there. [[Voice from the room: It works, yes.]] When the judge returns the case to rediscover it, he not only learns, but also knows the case very well. The instructions of the Board of Appeal are binding. It is not important to whom they are addressed, they are addressed to the court of first instance. There is another phenomenon the High House should know about. There are judges who, fearing the settlement of controversial matters, maybe unable or unwilling to judge in complex cases, requiring a lot of effort and decision-making sometimes with a high social impact, are ruling - I have to say this, although I am coming from the state of the judge - only in such a way as to get rid of the matter so that someone else can take responsibility for the decision, maybe the court of second instance in appeal, or maybe a colleague who will have bad luck and so badly done will have to decide again after retrial. Therefore, it is advisable that the case should return to the same judge, because only such a provision makes the temptation to avoid the outcome of the case not work. Unfortunately, we have statistics and they show that this phenomenon actually works. We must fight this, and if there are judges who are unable to cope with pressure or are unable to deal with difficult matters, they should consider their usefulness in this profession. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u572	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I'd like to say a few words about the new treatment of autism in children. Autism is a very sad disease because the affected child is excluded from the family. The name autism comes from the Greek word autos, which means: alone. The family feels that the baby isn't really connected to her, and I'm sure she would love to. It is also lonely and closed within itself. Therefore, various treatments for this disease are sought. Today's best method is psychotherapy, taming the child, showing him love and warmth. Unfortunately, it is a long-term process and it is actually not effective. In recent years, attempts have been made to treat the autism of their own stem cells, which, as it turns out, brings beneficial results. The best method is to use umbilical cells. umbilical cells are cells that are thrown into a bucket after birth. They can be drained from the umbilical cord, frozen and used in such situations. In 2017, a study on the use of cord stem cells in the treatment of autism was published in Stem Cells Journal. It has been shown that using them can bring beneficial results. The study was conducted in 25 children with autism spectrum disorder. The intravenous infusion of autologous umbilical blood has been shown to be very effective. This was confirmed and presented also at the conference in Warsaw in 2018. In the clinic that I have the privilege of running, in Cape Hope, we have undertaken such a test in Allan's boy and to our surprise, to be honest, it has indeed produced a very good result. A boy who had no contact with his family or his parents took quite a step forward, and his parents even thought it was a big step. Neurologists from the University Clinical Hospital in Lublin, who coordinate the treatment of such children on a national scale, are the same. If there are children in Poland with autism who have stored stem cells, then such an opportunity exists for them. Thank you very much for your attention. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u575	Mr Andrzej Melak, Club Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u576	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On 3 May 1919 on the slopes of the Citadel of Warsaw held the first celebration of independence of the Republic of Poland after 123 years of captivity. This feast took place not in palace halls or theatre halls, but on the slopes of the Warsaw Citadel, which was the place of many executions of Polish sons who wanted to cast off the yoke of Russian captivity. At this point, on 5 August 1864, the Russian assassin executed members of the National Government from the January Uprising with Romuald Traugutt at the head. On 3 May 1919, more than 140 thousand gathered here. residents of the Republic of Poland. There were: the government of the Republic, diplomatic corps, episcopate, representatives of diplomacy, crafts and numerous representatives of the Polish people. There was no radio or television at the time, but Poles waited over 120 years for this day and celebrated this day as they had decided. The Oak of Liberty and Independence was also planted here that day. There's been a half-morning Mass. It was attended by many veterans of the January Uprising, as well as two representatives from the time of the November Uprising. The oak of freedom and independence was destroyed, cut out during World War II. In 1993 the Katyn National Remembrance Circle with the participation of the Polish Episcopate, the army and numerous craftsmen in this place and with the participation of the army organized a repetition of this holiday. The Oak of Liberty was replanted, dug out of the Kawęczyn forest. A stone was placed on which the dates depicting the first bar confederation, the establishment of the Constitution on 3 May, 1918 as the date of regaining independence were written for Poles. On 2 May 2019 the Circle of National Remembrance organized again in the 100th anniversary of these celebrations, celebrations which gathered several hundred people. The Holy Mass was celebrated. with the participation of the parish priest of the local parish, the oldest parish in Warsaw. Prayers... Homily was given by Fr. Chaplain of General Sławomir Żarski. Fr. Waldemar Goncharuk. Numerous representatives of all groups and uniformed services involving military, police, professional and voluntary firefighters, railroaders, foresters' delegations from all over the country with flag mails took part in this ceremony. The fire brigade from Gąbina assisted in the Mass. ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u583	Mr Arkadiusz Mularczyk, Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u584	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal, it was really hard to listen to an earlier statement. Dear Marshal, Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, This year marks the 165th anniversary of the birth and 80th anniversary of the death of the legendary mayor of Nowy Sącz Bolesław Barbacki. He was born on 1 October 1854, in Słopnica Królewski district. He was a graduate of the judicial schools and then graduated from the Jagiellonian University in Krakow. From 1885 he led a stand-alone law firm in Nowy Sącz, and in 1896 he erected a house on the corner of the streets of Szwedzka and Dunajewski. In December 1914, Józef Piłsudski, who entered New Sącz at the head of the First Legion Brigade after the end of the Limanovsko-Marcinkowice campaign, settled in the House of Barbacks. The mayor was extremely proud of the fact that he could host a leading politician in his own home, with whom Poles had national hopes of freedom. Bolesław Barbacki held the office of Mayor of New Sącz for 18 years. In the memory of sądeczan remained as a great builder, the initiator of many new solutions important for the development of the city. He started, among others. The construction of modern waterworks and sewage system in Nowy Sącz, pushed through a resolution in Vienna obliging the owners of houses to build channels connecting them to the city or introduce a rent tax. Thanks to these revenues it was possible to change the appearance and character of the New Snot. Not everyone liked it then, and these actions of the mayor were recalled in 1918, and on their wave a group of extremists led to a situation in which the mayor of Barbacki resigned from his position. Despite the full discharge he received at that time, Władysław Barbacki expanded and rebuilt many streets, which then received electric lighting. In his day, many bilateral pavements were built, the market and Jagiellonian Street were paved. During Barbacki's reign, a modern city hospital was built for 200 beds and a school chapel. St. Kazimierz and the railway church. It was then that ornamental plants were developed, which were previously a wild garden. Since the time of Mayor Barbacki's plant, the city park has been named Tadeusz Kościuszko. During this period, the school was also built for them. Konarski, so-called. A small barracks, barracks for the local garrison, advance money and large meat establishments. Barbacki renovated the town hall building and the Jagiellonian castle. As an active activist of the Association of the Falcon and the Sodalition of Mariana, he supported independent organizations, among others. The Union of Armed Action, Scouting, the Union of Shooting and the District National Committee providing assistance to Polish Legions. At the initiative of Władysław Barbacki, the city council approved the support of the Legions with the amount of 100,000. crowns, of which Józef Piłsudski himself was grateful to mention in 1914 in Nowy Sącz. Władysław Barbacki was an honorary citizen of the city of Nowy Sącz and during his life he was evaluated as the most entrepreneurial mayor in the history of the city. One year after his death in 1939, in the district of Wólka, his name was named one of the main streets. Thank you very much, Marshal, for allowing me to make this message. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-06-13-sejm-82-2.u586	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! It is in Wieliczka that 44. World Balneological Congress and Congress of the Polish Society of Balneology and Physics Medicine. Wieliczka is a unique place not only because of the unique nature of the salt mine itself, but also because of the fact that it is here in Wieliczka that Prof. Mieczysław Skulimowski developed a method of treatment using the natural properties of the climate. Based on this method in 1958 Prof. Skulimowski created in Wieliczka the first microclimate underground sanatorium in Poland, where respiratory diseases are still being treated. In order to promote his achievements in this field in 1963, the professor founded the world's first scientific association of underground climate therapy, organized numerous scientific conferences and maintained international contacts. History circles a circle. Today, the Wieliczka Salt Mine is developing and continuing its underground healing activity and it is also an honor to return to the tradition of international cooperation activities, hosting prominent doctors and representatives of science, distinguished guests. Such a dynamic development of spa activities on each level would not have been possible if not for the support and recommendation of Prof. Irena Ponikowska, who is present at a conference that has just begun today. On behalf of the parliamentarians of the Greater Land, I would like to thank you for the invaluable contribution of work and efforts to ensure that this method of treatment can develop in the service of the whole society. I wish the doctors from more than 30 countries a fruitful meeting at the conference and a wonderful experience of their stay in Wieliczka. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
